DOC-Q13On React 19 the reactErrorHandler root hooks silence the ErrorBoundary features the page describesP1proven

Proposed issue docs-change

Say on the React error boundary page how reactErrorHandler and ErrorBoundary interact on React 19

Description

guides/react/features/error-boundary.mdx:21-53 shows a React 19 root with onCaughtError: Sentry.reactErrorHandler() next to Sentry.ErrorBoundary, then documents beforeCapture tags (:151) and captureReactException with the linked component stack (:173).

Observed with the page's own main.jsx: every boundary error is captured first by the root hook (mechanism auto.function.react.error_handler, handled: false), and the boundary's own capture, the beforeCapture tags and captureReactException are dropped by the dedupe integration, five times Not capturing exception because it's already been captured. On a plain createRoot without the hooks the boundary path works as described: auto.function.react.error_boundary, handled: true, tags.section set, component stack linked.

Related, on the guide itself: guides/react/index.mdx:163-167 passes a callback to onUncaughtError: Sentry.reactErrorHandler((error, errorInfo) => {...}); the render error that unmounted the whole root then arrives with handled: true, because passing a callback marks the error as handled, while the callback-less onCaughtError hook reports boundary-caught errors as handled: false.

What to change:

  • State that with onCaughtError set, the ErrorBoundary's beforeCapture and captureReactException never run, and tell readers who rely on them to leave onCaughtError out or to tag inside its callback.
  • Say what the handled flag means for each hook, and drop the console.warn callback from the onUncaughtError snippet or explain its effect.

DOC-Q28The esm-without-import page says a first-line import cannot instrument frameworks; Express, Fastify, Hapi and Hono are fully instrumented that wayP1proven

Proposed issue docs-change

Correct the first-line import limitation on the esm-without-import page for v11

Description

common/install/esm-without-import.mdx:28-30 says that with a direct first-line import only native Node.js APIs can be instrumented and the SDK will not capture framework-specific data. Observed on develop: import "./instrument.mjs" as the first line of the Express app produced exactly the --import output, with middleware and handler spans, route-named segments (GET /users/:id, source route) and auto.http.express error events; the Node run's Express check saw the same, and the Fastify run got the same hook and handler spans and auto.function.fastify events from a first-line import (QS-fastify, variant self-import). Koa is the counter-example: its first-line import produced url-named GET segments and no router or middleware span (QS-koa, variant self-import), so the page should name what is covered and what is not instead of a blanket warning. Hapi behaves like Fastify: the first-line import produced the same items as --import, every Hapi span, route name and error (QS-hapi, variant self-import). Hono too: sentry(app) patches the app instance at registration time, and the first-line import produced the same route-named segments, middleware spans and errors as --import (QS-hono, variant self-import).

What to change:

  • Turn the page into the bundler-plus-top-level-import method the SDK team settled on 2026-09-09 (DOC-Q85): say that a plain first-line import instruments only what registers at runtime (measured: Express, Fastify, Hapi and Hono yes, Koa no), show import "./instrument.mjs"; await import("./app.mjs"); as the bundler-free way to keep the init ahead of the framework, and name the bundler plugin as the way to instrument everything without --import; the NestJS copy of the page, guides/nestjs/install/esm-without-import.mdx:18-20, needs the same qualification, since the guide's default CommonJS build gets full Nest and Express spans from a first-line import (QS-nestjs, variant cjs).

Source: runtime docs audit, item DOC-Q28, quick start QS-express, variant self-import

What ran: Express getting-started reproduction on 2026-09-09, develop 03aaf42455 (@sentry/node 10.67.0, 46 integrations), express 4.22.2 and 5.2.1, Node 26.5.0: quickstarts/express, results-*.json per variant.

DOC-Q63The WebAssembly guide promises debug IDs and images that need a build_id section and a streaming loader it never mentionsP1proven

Proposed issue docs-change

Say on the WebAssembly guide what the integration needs: a build_id section, a URL-keeping loader and uploaded debug files

Description

guides/wasm/index.mdx:65 says the integration provides Debug IDs, Debug Files, Code IDs, Code Files and memory addresses. With a module that has no build_id custom section (nothing in the guide's install or Rust steps adds one; wasm-split does) the event carries only instruction_addr and platform: native on the wasm frame, no addr_mode and no debug_meta: the registry skips modules without build_id. With the section present the full set arrives (addr_mode: rel:0, a debug_meta.images entry of type wasm with code_id, code_file, debug_file and debug_id). The page never mentions wasm-split, build_id, DWARF or the debug file upload.

Loading also matters and the page (:160, :176-179) is silent on it: a module loaded with WebAssembly.instantiate(bytes) after fetch().arrayBuffer() is named wasm://wasm/<hash> by Chrome, so even with build_id no image matches and no addr_mode or debug_meta is attached; instantiateStreaming and compileStreaming keep the URL and match.

What to change:

  • State at :65 that the module must carry a build_id section (wasm-split) and that the DWARF debug file has to be uploaded, link the debug files docs, and add that symbolication needs a loader that keeps the module URL.

DOC-Q65The Elysia guide says client errors are not captured by default; unmatched routes and status() throws areP1proven

Proposed issue docs-change

Correct the default error filter description on the Elysia guide

Description

guides/elysia/index.mdx:139 says client errors (3xx and 4xx) are not captured by default. The default filter reads context.set.status, which is 200 for Elysia's NotFoundError (an unmatched route, 404 to the client) and 500 for anything thrown through status(4xx, ...): both are captured, Error: NOT_FOUND once per process because Elysia reuses one instance, and a status() throw as 'ElysiaCustomStatusResponse' captured as exception with keys: code, response. Only set.status = 4xx before a throw is skipped. The guide's own shouldHandleError snippet (:146-151) suppresses them.

What to change:

  • State that unmatched routes and status()-thrown responses are captured by default, that status() throws arrive as non-Error objects, and point at shouldHandleError as the filter.

Description

DOC-Q84Cloudflare Pages details: the nodejs_compat requirement depends on the date, request spans are method-named, waitUntil on PagesP2proven

Proposed issue docs-change

Add the missing Pages details to the Cloudflare guide and its config include

Description

platform-includes/getting-started-config/javascript.cloudflare.mdx:5 says the SDK needs the nodejs_compat flag and a compatibility date of 2024-09-23 or later. With no flags at all the same bundle runs at compatibility date 2026-09-01 (identical items) and fails at 2024-09-23 (No such module "node:async_hooks"); the requirement depends on the date, which the include does not say. Every Pages function-route segment is named GET with source url and no http.route; the parameterized functions/api/users/[id].js is GET with url.path /api/users/42, so Pages file routes are not visible as routes. What to change:

Checked, not a defect

N1Bun outgoing node:http and fetch requests are instrumented and propagatenot reproduced

gist B1 reported node:http clients uninstrumented on Bun. probes/PROP.mjs, PKG=@sentry/bun bun run PROP.mjs, bun 1.4.0, probes/results/PROP-bun.json: both /fetch and /http received sentry-trace and baggage, and both appear as http.client children of parent. Same as node. No docs change needed; DOC-B1 can say both clients are covered under bun run.

N2Importing @sentry/deno before the library does not block injectionnot reproduced

A first probe showed runtime: [] after await import('mysql') when @sentry/deno was imported first. The cause was the probe: mysql loads its instrumented lib/Connection.js on createConnection(), so the marker stays empty until the first connection. With that call (probes/D-HOOK2.mjs) the documented order (hook, SDK import, init(), library) injects on deno 2.9.0 and 2.8.3 (probes/results/D-HOOK.txt).

N3compatibility_date: 2024-09-23 with nodejs_compat boots the SDKnot reproduced

getting-started-config/javascript.cloudflare.mdx:5 states this floor. probes/CF/wrangler-olddate.jsonc returns the same nine integrations and propagated headers as a 2026 date. nodejs_als alone also boots (probes/results/CF-RUN.txt), so the include's claim is correct and DOC-C8 only asks the Next.js page to say its later date is OpenNext's.
